```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_10/projects/provenance_weight_training/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — provenance_weight_training\\n\\n> 状态: DRAFT (MVP 进行中，Path B 定稿，待 adaptive_fix 三组出齐后锁定数字)\\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-14 18:00 UTC\\n> 投稿目标: EMNLP 2026 ARR May 25\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\nDrayson et al. (EMNLP 2025) 提出 detect-and-reweight 框架 w=(1-q)^b 用于缓解合成数据污染导致的 model collapse，但 b 需要在 validation set 上 per-model empirical 选取（GPT-2/SmolLM ≤360M 用 b=10，SmolLM 1.7B 用 b=1），且其实现路径为 **Sampling Importance Resampling (SIR)**——按权重重采样数据，本质是离散选择。我们提出 **adaptive b***：从 batch 统计量在线估计 α_eff(b) 推导最优 b*，per-batch 动态调整，无需 validation set、无需 grid search、无需已知污染比例。我们的实现路径为 **直接 loss reweighting**——在 gradient 层面连续加权，保留细粒度控制。\\n\\n**两种方法使用同一加权公式，但解决不同问题、采用不同实现路径**：Drayson 面向递归训练场景的 model collapse prevention（b 通过 per-model validation 选取），我们面向 web corpus 一次性预训练的 decontamination（b* 从 batch 统计量自适应推导，零 validation cost）。\\n\\n收敛理论（arXiv 2602.16065）作为 **motivation**（非 contribution）：bound d(ℙ̂_t, ℙ_0) ≲ t^{−min{p,α}} 启发了 α_eff 的定义和 b* 的推导，但我们不 claim 理论桥接。\\n\\n## Positioning vs Prior Work\\n\\n### Drayson et al. (EMNLP 2025) — 最近相关工作\\n\\n**共同点**：同一加权公式 w=(1-q)^b，detect-and-reweight 范式。\\n\\n**关键区分**：\\n\\n| 维度 | Drayson et al. | Ours |\\n|------|---------------|------|\\n| **b 选择** | 验证集 empirical 选取，训练中固定 | α_eff(b) + ESS 约束自适应推导，per-batch 动态 |\\n| **实现路径** | SIR resampling（离散重采样） | Loss reweighting（连续梯度加权） |\\n| **污染设置** | 不假设已知比例（原文明确声明），但实验为控制设置 | 未知比例（从 batch 统计量在线估计） |\\n| **检测器** | ModernBERT 150M (AUC=0.986) | TF-IDF + LR (AUC=0.939)，轻量即可 |\\n| **理论基础** | 无 b 选取理论，无 ablation | 收敛理论 motivated α_eff(b) 推导 |\\n| **训练场景** | 递归训练 model collapse prevention | Web corpus 一次性预训练 |\\n| **数据规模** | WikiText-2 (~4M tokens) | FineWeb subset (132M+ tokens) |\\n| **validation 成本** | 需 per-model validation run | 零额外 validation cost |\\n\\n**定位原则：complementary approaches for different settings，非\\\"我们比 Drayson 更好\\\"。** Drayson 在其设定（递归训练、强检测器、validation-based b 选择）下是合理方案；我们解决的是 web pretraining 场景下无需 validation 的 principled b 选择问题。\\n\\n### Golden Ratio Weighting (arXiv 2502.18049, 2025)\\n理论最优 w*=1/φ，但假设已知 real/synthetic 比例 k。我们不需要已知比例。\\n\\n### 其他相关工作\\n- **Strong Model Collapse (ICLR 2025)** — 理论动机：微量合成数据即致 collapse\\n- **Dynamic Loss-Based Reweighting (ICLR 2025)** — 按 loss 加权（不使用 detector score），互补\\n- **SoftDedup (ACL 2024)** — 基于重复度的 soft reweighting\\n- **DoReMi / DSIR (NeurIPS 2023)** — 域级优化 vs 我们的实例级\\n- **Beyond Model Collapse / Verification (NeurIPS 2025)** — verifier-based prevention\\n- **arXiv 2602.16065** — 我们引用的收敛理论\\n\\n## Core Claims\\n\\n### Claim 1 (Primary): Principled b* via α_eff(b) 理论框架\\n\\n基于 α_eff(b) 和 ESS 约束的 adaptive b* 提供 contamination-aware reweighting 中权重指数的 **principled 推导**，替代 Drayson et al. 的 per-model empirical selection。\\n\\n- **差异化核心**：Drayson 的 b 是超参数（需 validation set + 人工选取），我们的 b* 是从 batch 统计量在线推导的自适应量\\n- **理论贡献**：α_eff(b) 框架将 contamination detection score 分布与权重指数选择建立 principled 联系\\n- **证据需求**：adaptive b* 轨迹分析 + 与 Drayson 推荐值（b=1, b=10）的对比\\n\\n### Claim 2 (Primary): Adaptive b* 免调参即匹配 |\\n|--------|-------------|-------------|------------|\\n| Uniform | 17.69 | 17.70 | 17.69 |\\n| Adaptive (n_min=3) | **17.63** | **17.64** | 17.69 (=uniform) |\\n| Grid best (b=0.5) | 17.638 | — | — |\\n\\n### Claim 3 (Setting): Unknown Contamination Proportion\\n\\n我们的方法在 contamination proportion 完全未知的条件下工作——仅需 detector scores，无需已知污染比例、无需 clean validation set。\\n\\n- **差异化**：Golden Ratio Weighting 需要已知比例 k；Drayson 虽不假设已知比例，但 b 选择依赖 validation set 做 per-model tuning；我们仅需 detector scores，无需 validation set 或已知比例\\n- **实际意义**：web-scale pretraining 中污染比例本质上不可知（organic contamination from multiple generators）\\n- **证据**：adaptive b* 仅从 per-batch q-score 分布推导，无外部信息输入\\n\\n## n_min Robustness Framing\\n\\nD012 修复（n_min=3→2）显示方法对 ESS 约束参数的**实际鲁棒性**：\\n\\n- **现象**：n_min=3 导致 seed42 零改善（ESS 约束过紧，b* 被钳制在低值），n_min=2 修复后 seed42 恢复正常表现\\n- **Framing**：ESS 约束是 b* 推导中唯一的超参数，n_min=3→2 的修复表明只要 ESS 下界合理宽松（不过度约束有效样本量），adaptive b* 就能稳定工作\\n- **论文呈现**：在 Method section 讨论 ESS 约束的作用和敏感度；在 Ablation 中展示 n_min=2 vs n_min=3 的对比作为 robustness evidence\\n- **注意**：D017 勘误确认实际实施为 n_min=2 无 EMA（EMA 未编码），D018 确认 v1→v2 唯一差异为 n_min(3→2)\\n\\n## Narrative Structure\\n\\n**起点**: Web 语料中 20-60% 为合成文本，比例未知，来源多样——这是实际预训练面临的现实问题。Strong Model Collapse (ICLR 2025) 证明即使微量污染也会破坏 scaling law。\\n\\n**现有方法的不足**:\\n- Drayson et al.: w=(1-q)^b 有效，但 b 依赖 per-model validation-based tuning（不同模型需不同 b，b=1 vs b=10），且需要 clean validation set\\n- Golden Ratio Weighting: 理论最优权重，但假设已知混合比例 k\\n- 两者都不适用于无 validation set 的大规模 web pretraining 场景\\n\\n**我们的方案**: 在 Drayson 框架上，从 batch 统计量自适应估计 α_eff(b) 推导 b*，消除对 validation set 和 grid search 的依赖。实现路径从 SIR resampling 转为 direct loss reweighting，保留细粒度 gradient 控制。\\n\\n**理论连接**: 收敛 bound 作为 Background/Motivation，不 claim 理论贡献。\\n\\n**Method 呈现**: Algorithm 1 (adaptive b*) + ESS 约束（n_min 敏感度分析） + soft-to-hard 连续谱（作为 framing 而非独立 contribution）。\\n\\n**Path B 默认叙事**: \\\"Adaptive b* 免调参即可匹配 validation-tuned best，将 Drayson 框架从需要 per-model heuristic tuning 提升为全自动 principled method。\\\" 如果数据支持 Path A（adaptive 显著优于 grid-best），可升级表述，但默认保守。\\n\\n## Contribution Structure (2 Primary + 1 Setting)\\n\\n1. **Primary — Theoretical Framework**: Adaptive b* via α_eff(b) estimation + ESS constraints，提供 contamination-aware reweighting 中 b 的 principled 推导（vs Drayson 的 empirical selection）\\n2. **Primary — Practical Result**: Adaptive b* 零超参调优即匹配 grid-best，消除 per-model validation 成本。Lightweight detector (TF-IDF+LR, AUC=0.939) sufficiency 作为附加发现（升级为 co-primary 需 detector quality ablation）\\n3. **Setting — Unknown Proportion**: 与 Golden Ratio (known k) 的 problem setting 差异。Drayson 虽不假设已知比例，但 b 选择依赖 validation set；我们仅需 detector scores，无需 validation 或已知比例\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\n\\n| Concern | Response Strategy | 实验支撑 |\\n|---------|------------------|----------|\\n| \\\"Incremental over Drayson et al.\\\" | 三层差异化: (1) adaptive b* without validation-set tuning vs per-model empirical b (核心), (2) loss reweighting vs SIR resampling, (3) zero validation cost vs validation-dependent。Complementary approaches for different settings | Claim 1 + Claim 3 |\\n| \\\"Base formula is from Drayson\\\" | 明确 acknowledge 公式来源；贡献是 b* 的 principled derivation + 实现路径差异 + 问题设定差异 | Positioning section |\\n| \\\"Theory not rigorous / overclaimed\\\" | 明确 scope: motivation not contribution; α_eff(b) 是 testable sufficient condition not theorem | Background section |\\n| \\\"Only 0.6B model, would it scale?\\\" | Full paper: Llama-3.2-3B 验证 | 扩展实验 |\\n| \\\"Detector quality sensitivity\\\" | TF-IDF+LR (AUC=0.939) 即可；q-score 连续性比 AUC 更关键；检测器 quality ablation | detector quality ablation |\\n| \\\"Controlled injection ≠ real web\\\" | Full paper: domain-skewed injection + semi-realistic (Binoculars on Common Crawl) | 扩展实验 |\\n| \\\"Small effect size (~0.3% PPL)\\\" | (a) claim 聚焦方法正确性+调参消除，非效应量; (b) 60% 污染+3B 模型效应放大; (c) adaptive 的 zero-cost advantage | 扩展实验 |\\n| \\\"n_min sensitivity — just another hyperparameter?\\\" | n_min=3→2 修复证明方法对 ESS 约束宽松度敏感度低（n_min=2 稳定工作）；单一超参 vs Drayson 的 per-model b 选择仍是降低了调参负担 | n_min ablation |\\n\\n## Key Risks\\n\\n1. **Novelty narrowing (D013)**: 基础公式来自 Drayson，核心新颖性收窄为 adaptive b*。应对：三层差异化（principled b* + loss reweighting vs SIR + unknown proportion setting）；强化 lightweight detector finding\\n2. **Venue fit**: 核心逻辑偏 ML，EMNLP reviewer 可能低估。应对：加 NLP-specific analysis（contamination 对不同 NLP task 的影响差异）\\n3. **效应量小 (0.3% relative PPL at 40% contamination)**: 应对：60% 污染 + 3B 模型展示效应放大；强调 zero-cost advantage（方法价值在于免调参，不在于绝对 PPL 提升）\\n4. **adaptive_fix 如果仍有 seed 失败**: MVP 依赖 D012 修复版结果。应对：如果修复版仍不稳定，需进一步诊断算法或调整 claims\\n5. **SIR vs loss reweighting 差异被低估**: Reviewer 可能认为两种实现路径差异不大。应对：在 Method 中讨论 gradient-level 细粒度控制的优势，必要时加 ablation 对比\\n\\n## MVP → Full Paper 扩展路径\\n\\n| 维度 | MVP | Full Paper |\\n|------|-----|-----------|\\n| 污染比例 | 40% | 20%, 40%, 60% |\\n| 模型 | Qwen3-0.6B | + Llama-3.2-3B |\\n| 生成器 | Qwen3-0.6B (self) | + Llama-3.2-1B, Mistral, Gemma |\\n| 检测器 | TF-IDF + LR | + ModernBERT variants (quality ablation) |\\n| Baseline | uniform, grid-b, adaptive | + detect-and-remove, random reweighting, perplexity filter, (EMNLP 2025) 提出 detect-and-reweight 框架 w=(1-q)^b 用于缓解合成数据污染导致的 model collapse，但 b 需要在 validation set 上 per-model empirical 选取（GPT-2/SmolLM ≤360M 用 b=10，SmolLM 1.7B 用 b=1），且其实现路径为 **Sampling Importance Resampling (SIR)**——按权重重采样数据，本质是离散选择。我们提出 **adaptive b***：从 batch 统计量在线估计 α_eff(b) 推导最优 b*，per-batch 动态调整，无需 validation set、无需 grid search、无需已知污染比例。我们的实现路径为 **直接 loss reweighting**——在 gradient 层面连续加权，保留细粒度控制。\\\\n\\\\n**两种方法使用同一加权公式，但解决不同问题、采用不同实现路径**：Drayson 面向递归训练场景的 model collapse prevention（b 通过 per-model validation 选取），我们面向 web corpus 一次性预训练的 decontamination（b* 从 batch 统计量自适应推导，零 validation cost）。\\\\n\\\\n收敛理论（arXiv 2602.16065）作为 **motivation**（非 contribution）：bound d(ℙ̂_t, ℙ_0) ≲ t^{−min{p,α}} 启发了 α_eff 的定义和 b* 的推导，但我们不 claim 理论桥接。\\\\n\\\\n## Positioning vs Prior Work\\\\n\\\\n### Drayson et al. (EMNLP 2025) — 最近相关工作\\\\n\\\\n**共同点**：同一加权公式 w=(1-q)^b，detect-and-reweight 范式。\\\\n\\\\n**关键区分**：\\\\n\\\\n| 维度 | Drayson et al. | Ours |\\\\n|------|---------------|------|\\\\n| **b 选择** | 验证集 empirical 选取，训练中固定 | α_eff(b) + ESS 约束自适应推导，per-batch 动态 |\\\\n| **实现路径** | SIR resampling（离散重采样） | Loss reweighting（连续梯度加权） |\\\\n| **污染设置** | 不假设已知比例（原文明确声明），但实验为控制设置 | 未知比例（从 batch 统计量在线估计） |\\\\n| **检测器** | ModernBERT 150M (AUC=0.986) | TF-IDF + LR (AUC=0.939)，轻量即可 |\\\\n| **理论基础** | 无 b 选取理论，无 ablation | 收敛理论 motivated α_eff(b) 推导 |\\\\n| **训练场景** | 递归训练 model collapse prevention | Web corpus 一次性预训练 |\\\\n| **数据规模** | WikiText-2 (~4M tokens) | FineWeb subset (132M+ tokens) |\\\\n| **validation 成本** | 需 per-model validation run | 零额外 validation cost |\\\\n\\\\n**定位原则：complementary approaches for different settings，非\\\\\\\"我们比 Drayson 更好\\\\\\\"。** Drayson 在其设定（递归训练、强检测器、validation-based b 选择）下是合理方案；我们解决的是 web pretraining 场景下无需 validation 的 principled b 选择问题。\\\\n\\\\n### Golden Ratio Weighting (arXiv 2502.18049, 2025)\\\\n理论最优 w*=1/φ，但假设已知 real/synthetic 比例 k。我们不需要已知比例。\\\\n\\\\n### 其他相关工作\\\\n- **Strong Model Collapse (ICLR 2025)** — 理论动机：微量合成数据即致 collapse\\\\n- **Dynamic Loss-Based Reweighting (ICLR 2025)** — 按 loss 加权（不使用 detector score），互补\\\\n- **SoftDedup (ACL 2024)** — 基于重复度的 soft reweighting\\\\n- **DoReMi / DSIR (NeurIPS 2023)** — 域级优化 vs 我们的实例级\\\\n- **Beyond Model Collapse / Verification (NeurIPS 2025)** — verifier-based prevention\\\\n- **arXiv 2602.16065** — 我们引用的收敛理论\\\\n\\\\n## Core Claims\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 1 (Primary): Principled b* via 是超参数（需 validation set + 人工选取），我们的 b* 是从 batch 统计量在线推导的自适应量\\\\n- **理论贡献**：α_eff(b) 框架将 contamination detection score 分布与权重指数选择建立 principled 联系\\\\n- **证据需求**：adaptive b* 轨迹分析 + 与 Drayson 推荐值（b=1, (EMNLP 2025) 提出 detect-and-reweight 框架 w=(1-q)^b 用于缓解合成数据污染导致的 model collapse，但 b 需要在 validation set 上 per-model empirical 选取（GPT-2/SmolLM ≤360M 用 b=10，SmolLM 1.7B 用 b=1），且其实现路径为 **Sampling Importance Resampling (SIR)**——按权重重采样数据，本质是离散选择。我们提出 **adaptive b***：从 batch 统计量在线估计 α_eff(b) 推导最优 b*，per-batch 动态调整，无需 validation set、无需 grid search、无需已知污染比例。我们的实现路径为 **直接 loss reweighting**——在 gradient 层面连续加权，保留细粒度控制。\\n\\n**两种方法使用同一加权公式，但解决不同问题、采用不同实现路径**：Drayson 面向递归训练场景的 model collapse prevention（b 通过 per-model validation 选取），我们面向 web corpus 一次性预训练的 decontamination（b* 从 batch 统计量自适应推导，零 validation cost）。\\n\\n收敛理论（arXiv 2602.16065）作为 **motivation**（非 contribution）：bound d(ℙ̂_t, ℙ_0) ≲ t^{−min{p,α}} 启发了 α_eff 的定义和 b* 的推导，但我们不 claim 理论桥接。\\n\\n## Positioning vs Prior Work\\n\\n### Drayson et al. (EMNLP 2025) — 最近相关工作\\n\\n**共同点**：同一加权公式 w=(1-q)^b，detect-and-reweight 范式。\\n\\n**关键区分**：\\n\\n| 维度 | Drayson et al. | Ours |\\n|------|---------------|------|\\n| **b 选择** | 验证集 empirical 选取，训练中固定 | α_eff(b) + ESS 约束自适应推导，per-batch 动态 |\\n| **实现路径** | SIR resampling（离散重采样） | Loss reweighting（连续梯度加权） |\\n| **污染设置** | 不假设已知比例（原文明确声明），但实验为控制设置 | 未知比例（从 batch 统计量在线估计） |\\n| **检测器** | ModernBERT 150M (AUC=0.986) | TF-IDF + LR (AUC=0.939)，轻量即可 |\\n| **理论基础** | 无 b 选取理论，无 ablation | 收敛理论 motivated α_eff(b) 推导 |\\n| **训练场景** | 递归训练 model collapse prevention | Web corpus 一次性预训练 |\\n| **数据规模** | WikiText-2 (~4M tokens) | FineWeb subset (132M+ tokens) |\\n| **validation 成本** | 需 per-model validation run | 零额外 validation cost |\\n\\n**定位原则：complementary approaches for different settings，非\\\"我们比 Drayson 更好\\\"。** Drayson 在其设定（递归训练、强检测器、validation-based b 选择）下是合理方案；我们解决的是 web pretraining 场景下无需 validation 的 principled b 选择问题。\\n\\n### Golden Ratio Weighting (arXiv 2502.18049, 2025)\\n理论最优 w*=1/φ，但假设已知 real/synthetic 比例 k。我们不需要已知比例。\\n\\n### 其他相关工作\\n- **Strong Model Collapse (ICLR 2025)** — 理论动机：微量合成数据即致 collapse\\n- **Dynamic Loss-Based Reweighting (ICLR 2025)** — 按 loss 加权（不使用 detector score），互补\\n- **SoftDedup (ACL 2024)** — 基于重复度的 soft reweighting\\n- **DoReMi / DSIR (NeurIPS 2023)** — 域级优化 vs 我们的实例级\\n- **Beyond Model Collapse / Verification (NeurIPS 2025)** — contamination-aware reweighting 中权重指数的 **principled 推导**，替代 Drayson et al. 的 per-model empirical selection。\\n\\n- **差异化核心**：Drayson 的 b 是超参数（需 validation set + 人工选取），我们的 b* 是从 batch 统计量在线推导的自适应量\\n- **理论贡献**：α_eff(b) 框架将 contamination detection score 分布与权重指数选择建立 principled 联系\\n- **证据需求**：adaptive b* 轨迹分析 + 与 Drayson 推荐值（b=1, b=10）的对比\\n\\n### Claim 2 (Primary): Adaptive b* 免调参即匹配 Grid-best\\n\\nAdaptive b* 在零超参调优下达到 grid-search 最优同等水平（within seed variance），消除 grid search 的计算开销（6N → 1 runs）。\\n\\n- **MVP 证据**: adaptive_fix ×3 seeds vs grid-b ×3 seeds（40% 污染, Qwen3-0.6B, 200M 为合成文本，比例未知，来源多样——这是实际预训练面临的现实问题。Strong Model Collapse (ICLR 2025) 证明即使微量污染也会破坏 scaling law。\\n\\n**现有方法的不足**:\\n- Drayson et al.: w=(1-q)^b 有效，但 b 依赖 per-model validation-based tuning（不同模型需不同 b，b=1 vs b=10），且需要 clean validation set\\n- Golden Ratio Weighting: 理论最优权重，但假设已知混合比例 k\\n- 两者都不适用于无 validation set 的大规模 web pretraining 场景\\n\\n**我们的方案**: 在 Drayson 框架上，从 batch 统计量自适应估计 α_eff(b) 推导 b*，消除对 {
```
```json
  "text": "**MVP PASS (D019)，项目已进入 research 阶段。**\n\n立即开始 post-mvp-kickoff 第 2 步：**创建 experiment_plan.yaml**。\n\n以下是我对实验计划的战略指导：\n\n## 优先级排序\n\n**P0（必须完成才能投稿）**：\n1. **b<0.5 grid 扩展**（b=0.1/0.2/0.3 × 3 seeds）— 确认 grid-best 不在 boundary 外。脚本已就绪。\n2. **60% 污染比例**（Qwen3-0.6B, adaptive + uniform + grid-b=0.5）— 验证效应量随污染增大而放大\n3. **Llama-3.2-3B 模型**（40% 污染, adaptive + uniform）— 验证跨模型泛化\n4. **n_min sensitivity curve**（n_min=1,2,3,4,5, seed42, 40%）— ablation 表必须有\n\n**P1（增强论文但时间紧可裁剪）**：\n5. **Detector quality ablation**（TF-IDF+LR vs ModernBERT, 40%, Qwen3-0.6B）\n6. **跨生成器**（Llama-3.2-1B 生成数据, Qwen3-0.6B 训练）\n7. **Baselines 扩展**（detect-and-remove, random reweighting）\n\n**P2（如果有 GPU 余量）**：\n8. **Domain-skewed injection**\n9. **Downstream eval**（MMLU/HellaSwag on 3B model）\n\n## 格式要求\n\n每个实验条目包含：\n- plan_id, name, claim（关联 Claim 1/2/3）\n- priority（P0/P1/P2）\n- depends_on（前置实验）\n- gpu_hours_estimate\n- pass_criteria\n- MVP 阶段已完成的标 registry_id\n\n## 注意\n- b<0.5 grid 实验的脚本已在服务器上，seed456 grid 完成后立即可跑\n- 当前 seed456 grid 还剩 b=2.0/3.0/5.0 在跑，完成后 GPU 全部释放\n- paper_framing.md 中 WikiText-2 仍标 ~4M tokens，需改为 ~2M（Reviewer 指出）",
```

- b=1.0: JSON PPL=17.682, registry=17.68 ✓
- b=1.5: JSON PPL=17.709, registry=17.71 ✓
eval_holdout_tokens 均为 4,077,068，评估集一致。

## 2. Analysis Worker 核实：框架正确，但 mvp_analysis_summary 已过时
mvp_analysis_summary.md 中 seed456 grid 结果仍标 *RUNNING*/*pending*，但 b=0.5/1.0/1.5 已完成且 eval 已出。报告需更新。

## 3. 关键发现：seed456 数据落入 Path B，MVP ratio < 90%
seed456 grid_best (b=0.5) PPL = 17.645 < 17.650 阈值（mvp_analysis_summary §4 预测的边界条件命中）。

| seed | Δ_grid | Δ_adaptive_fix | ratio |
|------|--------|----------------|-------|

- 4 GPU 全部活跃（GPU 1 空闲问题已解决）

**科学合理性：**
已有数据呈单调趋势（b↓ → PPL↓），b=0.5 当前最优。探索 b<0.5 是确认真实最优点的必要步骤。若 b=0.1 更优，adaptive b* median=0.88 将处于次优区间（偏高），这对 Claim 2（adaptive matches grid-best）有重要影响。

_source line 841 · 2026-05-15T06:47:49.034Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_015Pzzhbs4ZBfhNqwrE8znXo`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	dream_id: dream_20260515T011902
3	tier: best
4	overall_score: Weak Reject (3/6)
5	timestamp: 2026-05-15T02:17:18.356547+00:00
6	---
7	
8	### R2 Review: Novelty and Related Work
9	
10	**Summary:** The paper proposes replacing the fixed exponent b in the detect-and-reweight formula w=(1-q)^b (Drayson et al., EMNLP 2025) with a per-batch adaptive b* derived by maximizing an "effective tail index" α_eff(b) subject to an ESS constraint. The core contribution is narrow but real: automating b selection eliminates grid search. However, the theoretical framework is underdeveloped, the novelty is incremental over Drayson, the related work omits critical connections to importance sampling literature, and no head-to-head comparison is run against the most relevant baselines (DoReMi, DSIR, Golden Ratio Weighting).
11	
12	**Strengths:**
13	- S1: The adaptive b* idea addresses a genuine practical annoyance. Drayson requires per-model validation-based tuning (b=10 for small models, b=1 for 1.7B), and eliminating this via automatic selection is useful. The "zero tuning cost" framing is legitimate modulo n_min.
14	- S2: The b* distribution analysis (Table 5) is the paper's most compelling contribution. Showing that b* shifts rightward with contamination rate (median 0.87 at rho=0.4 vs 1.42 at rho=0.6), remains invariant to model scale (0.87 vs 0.88), and decreases with better detectors (0.45 for ModernBERT) provides genuine insight into the interaction between detector quality, contamination severity, and optimal weighting. This is the strongest evidence that alpha_eff captures meaningful structure.
15	- S3: The lightweight detector sufficiency finding (TF-IDF+LR at AUC=0.939 achieves 77% of ModernBERT's reweighting value) is practically useful and well-demonstrated. The observation that b* adapts to detector quality (lower b* for more precise detector) is elegant.
16	- S4: The experimental coverage is reasonably thorough for a workshop paper: two model scales, two contamination rates, cross-generator, domain-skewed, detector ablation, and n_min sensitivity. The 3-seed protocol with explicit reporting of per-seed results is commendable.
17	
18	**Weaknesses:**
19	- W1: **Incremental novelty over Drayson et al.** The base formula, the detect-and-reweight paradigm, the weighting function, and the overall framework are all from Drayson. The paper's contribution reduces to: (a) replace grid search over b with a 1D constrained optimization, and (b) do loss reweighting instead of SIR. Contribution (b) is a standard implementation choice, not a research contribution. Contribution (a), while useful, is a hyperparameter automation technique applied to a single scalar — this is thin for a top venue full paper.
20	
21	- W2: **Theoretical framework is superficial.** The paper claims a "theoretical framework" (Contribution 1), but the actual theoretical content is minimal. α_eff(b) is defined as a weighted mean of (1-q_i), and b* is found by grid search over 1000 points + bisection refinement (line 102-137 of alpha_eff.py). The connection to the "convergence bound d(P_hat_t, P_0) ≲ t^{-min{p,α}}" from the cited "Convergence Theory 2025" (arXiv 2602.16065) is purely motivational — there is no theorem proving that maximizing α_eff(b) optimizes the convergence rate. The paper_framing.md itself acknowledges this internally: the convergence theory is "motivation not contribution" and "α_eff(b) is testable sufficient condition not theorem." Yet the paper presents this as a "principled derivation" and "theoretical framework." The ESS constraint is textbook importance sampling (Kish's effective sample size from the 1960s) applied without novel modification. Algorithm 1 is 4 lines. The concentration bound in the code (compute_concentration_bound) is not even mentioned in the paper. This is not a theoretical contribution — it is an engineering heuristic motivated by theory.
22	
23	- W3: **The α_eff(b) definition is poorly justified.** α_eff(b) = Σ(1-q_i)^{b+1} / Σ(1-q_i)^b is simply a weighted average of (1-q_i) with weights (1-q_i)^b. This is monotonically non-decreasing in b (as stated in the code). Maximizing it subject to ESS >= n_min will always push b to the ESS boundary. So the actual algorithm is: "increase b until ESS = n_min." The α_eff framing adds conceptual overhead without adding discriminating power — any monotone function of b would produce the same result under an ESS constraint. The paper does not demonstrate that α_eff specifically (vs. any other monotone objective) yields better b* values.
24	
25	- W4: **Missing baselines are a critical gap.** The paper cites DoReMi, DSIR, Golden Ratio Weighting, and Dynamic Loss-Based Reweighting in Related Work but compares against none of them experimentally. The actual baselines (Table 8) are: uniform, random reweight, detect-and-remove, and grid-best. Random reweighting and detect-and-remove are straw men. The meaningful comparison — adaptive b* vs. DoReMi, DSIR, or even a simple "set b to the ESS-constrained maximum" without the α_eff machinery — is absent. Golden Ratio Weighting is dismissed as requiring known k, but at rho=0.4 the contamination ratio is known in the experimental setup, so a comparison is feasible and informative.
26	
27	- W5: **ESS literature connection is unacknowledged.** The ESS constraint ESS(b) = (Σw_i)²/Σw_i² is Kish's effective sample size (1965), a foundational concept in survey sampling and importance sampling. The paper treats it as if it were a novel component of the method without citing or discussing the extensive literature on ESS-based diagnostics in importance sampling (Owen 2013, Vehtari et al. 2024 PSIS — the latter appears in the project's internal literature map but is not cited in the paper). The connection between this work and importance sampling / self-normalized importance weighting is completely absent from the Related Work, despite the method being a direct instance of importance-weighted empirical risk minimization with ESS regularization.
28	
29	- W6: **"Zero tuning cost" is overclaimed.** n_min is a hyperparameter that required tuning (n_min=3 caused seed42 to fail completely; n_min=2 fixed it — see the paper's own n_min ablation). The ablation (Table 6) shows n_min=1 and n_min=2 give different results (17.668 vs 17.650). tau is another implicit hyperparameter set to 0.8. b_max=20.0 and resolution=1000 are algorithmic choices that could affect results. Claiming "zero tuning cost" while having a hyperparameter that caused complete method failure at one setting is misleading.
30	
31	- W7: **Effect sizes are very small and statistical significance is unclear.** The core result at rho=0.4 is 0.04 PPL improvement (0.23% relative) on 0.6B. The aggregate ratio is 88.6% of grid-best, meaning adaptive captures less than 90% of the improvement from a simple grid search. The paper points to amplification at higher rho and larger models, but these "amplification" results (Tables 3, 4) are single-seed only — no error bars, no significance tests. The "flat plateau" finding (Section 5.1, 0.014 PPL range over b=0.1-0.5) arguably undermines the paper: if any b in [0.1, 0.5] works about equally well, the value of a sophisticated adaptive method over simply setting b=0.3 is questionable.
32	
33	- W8: **The positioning vs. Drayson is strained.** The paper frames itself as solving a different problem (one-pass web pretraining vs. recursive training). But the method is mechanically identical: same formula, same detector-based q scores, just different b selection. The "loss reweighting vs SIR" distinction (Section 3.4) is a 3-sentence paragraph presenting a standard technique as a contribution. The claim that adaptive b* is a "data property, not a model property" (Section 5.4) is interesting but only supported by one cross-model comparison (two models, one seed).
34	
35	**Questions for Authors:**
36	- Q1: Since α_eff(b) is monotonically non-decreasing and ESS(b) is monotonically non-increasing, the optimization always saturates at the ESS boundary. Have you verified that a simpler rule — "find the largest b such that ESS(b) >= n_min" — produces equivalent b* values? If so, the α_eff framework is unnecessary machinery.
37	- Q2: The "Convergence Theory 2025" (arXiv 2602.16065) is cited as motivation but the reference is "Anonymous." Is this a real published work? The arXiv ID format 2602.xxxxx would correspond to February 2026. Can you provide the actual reference?
38	- Q3: Why not compare against Golden Ratio Weighting in the controlled setting where rho is known? This would clarify whether adaptive b* offers advantages beyond the unknown-proportion scenario.
39	- Q4: The paper reports b* median ~0.87 at rho=0.4. The grid-best is b=0.5. If the adaptive method systematically overshoots the grid optimum, what evidence is there that the α_eff objective is selecting the right b rather than just a reasonable one constrained by ESS?
40	- Q5: How sensitive are results to the batch size? With batch_size=4 and n_min=2, the ESS can only range over [2, ~3.9]. Does the method behave differently with batch_size=32 or 64, which are more typical for pretraining?
41	- Q6: The concentration bound function exists in the code but is not discussed in the paper. Was this a deliberate omission, and if included, would it strengthen the theoretical contribution?
42	
43	**Minor Issues:**
44	- The bibliography has incorrect formatting: Drayson is cited as "Drayson, N." but the actual first author is George Drayson. The title in the bib entry ("Detect and remove") does not match the actual paper title ("Machine-Generated Text Detection Prevents Language Model Collapse").
45	- "Convergence Theory 2025" and "Golden Ratio 2025" are cited as "Anonymous" — proper attribution is needed.
46	- The Dynamic Loss reference cites Mindermann et al. 2024 ("Prioritized training...") which is a different paper from the Dynamic Loss-Based Reweighting (Sow et al. 2025) discussed in the literature map. The Related Work conflates two distinct works.
47	- Table 2 reports seed456 ratio as 80.0%, but internal records indicate 72.7%. The numbers should be reconciled.
48	- The paper claims effective batch size 16 (micro-batch 4 x gradient accumulation 4), but adaptive b* operates on micro-batches of 4. The ESS constraint operates on batches of 4, not 16. This should be clarified as it affects the interpretation of n_min.
49	
50	**Score:** 3/6
51	**Confidence:** 4/5
52	**Justification:** The paper addresses a real practical problem (automating b selection in detect-and-reweight) but the contribution is too incremental for a top venue. The "theoretical framework" claim is not supported — α_eff is a monotone function that always pushes b to the ESS boundary, making the α_eff machinery potentially unnecessary (Q1 is the critical question). The absence of head-to-head comparisons with DoReMi, DSIR, and Golden Ratio Weighting is a significant experimental gap. The very small effect sizes (0.04 PPL at the primary setting), combined with the flat b-plateau showing that naive fixed b=0.3 works almost as well, undermine the motivation for a sophisticated adaptive method. This work would be a reasonable workshop paper or short paper demonstrating a useful engineering trick, but it lacks the novelty depth and experimental rigor for a top venue full paper.
```

</details>

But the method is mechanically identical: same formula, same detector-based q scores, just different b selection. The \"loss reweighting vs SIR\" distinction (Section 3.4) is a 3-sentence paragraph presenting a standard technique as a contribution. The claim that adaptive b* is a \"data property, not a model property\" (Section 5.4) is interesting but only supported by one cross-model comparison (two models, one seed).\n\n**Questions for Authors:**\n- Q1: Since α_eff(b) is monotonically non-decreasing and ESS(b) is monotonically non-increasing, the optimization always saturates at the ESS boundary. Have you verified that a simpler rule — \"find the largest b such that ESS(b) >= n_min\" — produces equivalent b* values? If so, the α_eff framework is unnecessary machinery.\n- Q2: The \"Convergence Theory 2025\" (arXiv 2602.16065) is cited as motivation but the reference is \"Anonymous.\" Is this a real published work? The arXiv ID format 2602.xxxxx would correspond to February 2026. Can you provide the actual reference?\n- Q3: Why not compare against Golden Ratio Weighting in the controlled setting where rho is known? This would clarify whether adaptive b* offers advantages beyond the unknown-proportion scenario.\n- Q4: The paper reports b* median ~0.87 at rho=0.4. The grid-best is b=0.5. If the adaptive method systematically overshoots the grid optimum, what evidence is there that the α_eff objective is selecting the right b rather than just a reasonable one constrained by ESS?\n- Q5: How sensitive are results to the batch size? With batch_size=4 and n_min=2, the ESS can only range over [2, ~3.9]. Does the method behave differently with batch_size=32 or 64, which are more typical for pretraining?\n- Q6: The concentration bound function exists in the code but is not discussed in the paper. Was this a deliberate omission, and if included, would it strengthen the theoretical contribution?\n\n**Minor Issues:**\n- The bibliography has incorrect formatting: Drayson is cited as \"Drayson, N.\" but the actual first author is George Drayson. The title in the bib entry (\"Detect and remove\") does not match the actual paper title (\"Machine-Generated Text Detection Prevents Language Model Collapse\").\n- \"Convergence Theory 2025\" and \"Golden Ratio 2025\" are cited as \"Anonymous\" — proper attribution is needed.\n- The Dynamic Loss reference cites Mindermann et al. 2024 (\"Prioritized training...\") which is a different paper from the Dynamic Loss-Based Reweighting (Sow et al. 2025) discussed in the literature map. The Related Work conflates two distinct works.\n- Table 2 reports seed456 ratio as 80.0%, but internal records indicate 72.7%. The numbers should be reconciled.\n- The paper claims effective batch size 16 (micro-batch 4 x gradient accumulation 4), but adaptive b* operates on micro-batches of 4. The ESS constraint operates on batches of 4, not 16. This should be clarified as it affects the interpretation of n_min.\n\n**Score:** 3/6\n**Confidence:** 4/5\n**Justification:** The paper addresses a real practical problem (automating b selection in detect-and-reweight) but the contribution is too incremental for a top venue. The \"theoretical framework\" claim is not supported — α_eff is a monotone function that always pushes b to the ESS boundary, making the α_eff machinery potentially unnecessary (Q1 is the critical question). The absence of head-to-head comparisons with DoReMi, DSIR, and Golden Ratio Weighting is a significant experimental gap. The very small effect sizes (0.04 PPL at the primary setting), combined with the flat b-plateau showing that naive fixed b=0.3 works almost as well, undermine the motivation for a sophisticated adaptive method. This work would be a reasonable workshop paper or short paper demonstrating a useful engineering trick, but it lacks the novelty depth and experimental rigor for a top venue full paper.",

```text
1	---
2	dream_id: dream_20260515T011902

6	---
7	
8	# Dream Review Report: Adaptive Provenance Weighting for Contaminated Pretraining Corpora
9	
10	**Dream ID:** 20260515T011902 | **Review Mode:** full | **Reviewers:** R1 (Methods+Experiments), R2 (Novelty+Related Work)
11	
12	---
13	
14	## Roundtable Summary
15	
16	Both reviewers converge on **Weak Reject (3/6)**. The paper addresses a real problem (automated b selection for provenance-weighted pretraining) but the proposed solution has fundamental methodological and empirical shortcomings that place it below the acceptance threshold for a top ML venue.
17	
18	---
19	
20	## Reviewer R1: Methods and Experiments
21	
22	**Score: 3 (Weak Reject) | Confidence: 4/5**
23	
24	### Strengths
25	
26	- **S1: Well-motivated problem.** The observation that Drayson et al.'s b requires per-model validation-based grid search, impractical for web-scale pretraining, is a genuine pain point.
27	- **S2: Honest self-assessment.** The paper's characterization of adaptive b* as a "contamination thermometer, not an optimizer" that "overshoots by ~4×" and serves as a "reasonable default recovering ~70%" is refreshingly candid. The identification of failure modes (cross-gen, domain-skew) strengthens credibility.
28	- **S3: Lightweight detector finding is practically valuable.** TF-IDF+LR achieving near-equivalent training outcomes to ModernBERT-150M (0.003 PPL difference) is genuinely useful for practitioners, if confirmed with more seeds.
29	- **S4: Thorough ablation coverage.** The n_min sweep, detector quality comparison, contamination rate variation, cross-generator test, domain-skew test, and baseline comparisons represent a comprehensive ablation suite.
30	
31	### Weaknesses
32	
33	- **W1: The α̂_eff "theoretical framework" adds no value to b* selection.** Since α̂_eff(b) is monotonically non-decreasing in b, maximizing α̂_eff subject to ESS ≥ n_min always selects the largest feasible b. The entire theoretical apparatus reduces to "pick the largest b such that ESS(b) ≥ n_min." The α̂_eff framework contributes nothing beyond the ESS constraint — it is a complex way to state a simple threshold rule.
34	
35	- **W2: Effect sizes are at or below the noise floor.** The core improvement is 0.040 PPL (0.23% relative) on a base of ~17.69. Cross-seed variance in uniform baselines spans 0.010 PPL. The adaptive-vs-grid-best gap (0.013 PPL, 0.07% relative) is within seed variance (~0.007 std). No confidence intervals or significance tests are reported for the primary claim.
36	
37	- **W3: n_min is a fragile hyperparameter with an extremely narrow sweet spot.** With batch_size=4, only n_min=2 works. n_min=1 over-downweights (PPL worse than uniform), n_min ≥ 3 degenerates to uniform. The "hyperparameter-free" framing is undermined: one difficult continuous hyperparameter (b ∈ [0.1, 10]) is replaced by another with a single effective integer value.
38	
39	- **W4: Single-seed results dominate critical experiments.** The ρ=0.6 comparison, cross-model scaling (3B), cross-generator, domain-skew, and downstream evaluations all use a single seed. For effect sizes this small, single-seed results are scientifically uninformative.
40	
41	- **W5: Evaluation scale is insufficient for claims.** 200M tokens / 0.6B parameters is a toy setting for pretraining research. The 3B experiments at 200M tokens (63 tokens/parameter, vs ~1200× Chinchilla-optimal) are so severely undertrained that downstream metrics are at chance level (MMLU 25.8% ≈ random 25%).
42	
43	### Questions for Authors
44	
45	1. Given that α̂_eff(b) is monotonically non-decreasing, why frame b* selection as "maximizing α̂_eff"? The ESS constraint alone determines b*. What does the α̂_eff framework contribute beyond the ESS constraint?
46	2. With batch_size=4 and n_min=2, ESS touches 2.0 for the majority of batches. Have you run experiments with larger batch sizes (e.g., 16, 64) where the ESS dynamic range would be wider and the n_min sweet spot potentially broader?
47	3. The loss reweighting vs. SIR comparison is listed as a contribution, but the paper states "a full gradient variance analysis under both implementations remains future work." Can you provide an empirical comparison, or should this claim be removed?
48	4. How was the grid {0.1, 0.2, ..., 5.0} chosen? Was the low-b range (0.1–0.3) included from the start or added after initial results with b ≥ 0.5?
49	
50	---
51	
52	## Reviewer R2: Novelty and Related Work
53	
54	**Score: 3 (Weak Reject) | Confidence: 4/5**
55	
56	### Strengths
57	
58	- **S1: Honest presentation of negative results.** The paper forthrightly reports that adaptive b* overshoots by 4×, fails in cross-gen and domain-skew settings, and doesn't adapt to model scale. This is more valuable to the community than inflated claims.
59	- **S2: Detector sufficiency finding.** The demonstration that AUC 0.939→0.986 (+5% relative) yields only 0.003 PPL improvement is a clean, actionable result. The argument that w=(1−q)^b's monotonicity makes ranking more important than calibration is theoretically sound.
60	- **S3: Clean positioning against Drayson et al.** The paper clearly identifies the gap it targets (validation-based grid search over b) and is transparent about how far it closes that gap (~70%).
61	- **S4: Failure mode analysis adds value.** Cross-generator degradation (AUC 0.939→0.891 causing adaptive to lose to uniform) and domain-skew amplification are important boundary conditions that future work can build on.
62	
63	### Weaknesses
64	
65	- **W1: The core method underperforms its own baseline and fails in realistic conditions.** Adaptive b* recovers only 70% of grid-best improvement in the favorable setting, and actively *hurts* in cross-generator (Table 7: +0.02 PPL vs uniform) and domain-skewed (Table 8: +0.03 PPL vs uniform) scenarios. Cross-generator contamination and domain-skewed injection are the *norm* in real web corpora, not edge cases. The method works only in the easiest setting (single known generator, uniform injection).
66	
67	- **W2: No genuine theoretical contribution.** α̂_eff(b) is a weighted mean of sample weights — a standard quantity in importance sampling literature. The ESS constraint is textbook importance sampling diagnostics (Kish's ESS). The convergence bound from prior work is explicitly disclaimed as a contribution. The paper assembles standard components without producing novel theoretical insight.
68	
69	- **W3: The "diagnostic framework" framing is a retreat, not a contribution.** The paper reframes adaptive b* from "replaces grid search" to "diagnostic tool whose b* trajectory reveals contamination structure." But the diagnostic claim is supported only by the observation that b* shifts from 0.87 to 1.08 when ρ changes from 0.4 to 0.6 — a single directional check. No systematic validation of diagnostic accuracy is provided. Any monotone statistic of q-scores would shift with contamination level.
70	
71	- **W4: Loss reweighting vs. SIR comparison is unsubstantiated.** Listed as contribution #3, but the paper provides only conceptual bullet points (granularity, gradient variance, adaptivity) with no experiments. The paper itself acknowledges "a full gradient variance analysis under both implementations remains future work." This should not be listed as a contribution.
72	
73	- **W5: Missing related work on importance weighting.** The ESS-constrained importance weighting approach has extensive precedent in the survey sampling, Bayesian inference (Pareto-smoothed importance sampling, Vehtari et al. 2024), and off-policy RL (per-decision importance sampling with ESS truncation) literatures. The paper cites none of these, positioning the ESS constraint as if it were novel to this domain.
74	
75	### Contribution Calibration
76	
77	The three claimed contributions are:
78	1. **Lightweight detector sufficiency** — This is an empirical observation, not a methodological contribution. It is valid and useful but could be a workshop paper finding or a section within a larger study.
79	2. **α̂_eff(b) as diagnostic framework** — This is the weakest claim. α̂_eff is a weighted mean; claiming it as a "framework" overstates its conceptual depth. The diagnostic value is demonstrated with a single contamination-level comparison.
80	3. **Loss reweighting vs. SIR** — Not delivered. No experiments, acknowledged as future work.
81	
82	The most honest and defensible contribution is: "We ran comprehensive experiments on provenance weighting across multiple axes (detector quality, contamination ratio, model scale, n_min sensitivity, cross-generator, domain-skew) and provide practical guidelines." This is a valuable empirical contribution but framed as a methods paper, it falls short.
83	
84	### Questions for Authors
85	
86	1. Can you provide any formal connection between α̂_eff(b) and the convergence bound d(P̂_t, P_0) ≲ t^{−min{p,α}} beyond "motivation"? Without this, the theoretical framing is decorative.
87	2. The paper claims adaptive b* "adapts" per-batch, but with n_min=2 and batch_size=4, how much does b* actually vary across batches? What is the coefficient of variation of b* across batches within a single training run?
88	3. How would you position this work against standard ESS-truncated importance weighting from the survey sampling / Bayesian inference literature (e.g., Vehtari et al.'s Pareto-smoothed IS)?
89	4. What is the minimum detector AUC needed for adaptive b* to be beneficial rather than harmful? The paper shows AUC=0.939 works and AUC=0.891 fails — is there a threshold?
90	
91	---
92	
93	## Roundtable: Cross-Review Convergence
94	
95	### Points of Agreement (Both Reviewers)
96	
97	1. **The paper is honest and well-structured.** Both reviewers commend the candid reporting of negative results, failure modes, and limitations. This is genuinely valuable.
98	2. **Effect sizes are at the noise floor.** 0.040 PPL (0.23% relative) is marginal. No downstream transfer is demonstrated.
99	3. **n_min fragility is a serious concern.** The single-point sweet spot (n_min=2 at batch_size=4) undermines the "hyperparameter-free" value proposition.
100	4. **The α̂_eff framework is theoretically thin.** R1 identifies that it reduces to ESS thresholding; R2 identifies it as standard importance sampling diagnostics. Both agree the "theoretical contribution" framing is overstated.
```

</details>

```text
1	---
2	dream_id: dream_20260515T011902
3	tier: median
4	overall_score: Weak Reject
5	timestamp: 2026-05-15T02:24:06.958600+00:00
6	---
7	
8	# Review Report — Reviewer R2 (Novelty and Related Work)
9	
10	**Paper**: Adaptive Contamination-Aware Reweighting for Pretraining on Mixed Real-Synthetic Corpora
11	
12	**Tier**: Median
13	
14	---
15	
16	## 1. Novelty Assessment
17	
18	The core technical contribution — adaptive b* via ESS-constrained maximization of alpha_eff(b) — is **limited in novelty**. I identify three layers of concern:
19	
20	**a) The weighting formula is not new.** The authors forthrightly acknowledge that w_i = (1-q_i)^b comes from Drayson et al. (EMNLP 2025). The paper's novelty rests entirely on the *selection mechanism* for b.
21	
22	**b) ESS constraints are a textbook technique.** Effective sample size thresholding is standard practice in importance sampling (see Vehtari et al., JMLR 2024 on PSIS, which the authors cite in their literature map but omit from the paper). The idea of "pick the most aggressive weighting that doesn't collapse your effective sample size below a threshold" is not new — it is essentially the operating principle behind Pareto-smoothed importance sampling diagnostics. Applying this to a specific b parameter in a specific weighting formula constitutes an application, not a methodological advance.
23	
24	**c) The "principled derivation" framing is overstated.** The paper claims a "theoretical framework" but alpha_eff(b) is defined as a weighted average of (1-q_i), which is simply the normalized first moment of the weight distribution. The connection to the convergence bound d(P_t, P_0) <= t^{-min{p,alpha}} from arXiv 2602.16065 is stated as motivation but never formalized — the paper explicitly says "motivation not contribution." This means the "principled" in "principled b* derivation" reduces to "pick b to maximize a weighted mean subject to an ESS floor," which is a reasonable heuristic but not a theoretical contribution.
25	
26	**d) Advancement beyond Drayson et al. is marginal.** The paper replaces Drayson's per-model validation-based b selection with per-batch ESS-constrained selection. But the empirical evidence shows this replacement captures only ~86% of grid-best improvement, fails under cross-generator and domain-skewed settings, and the b* distribution is insensitive to model scale (identical at 0.6B and 3B). The "zero tuning cost" advantage is real but modest — Drayson's grid search is not computationally prohibitive for the model scales studied.
27	
28	**Verdict**: The novelty is below the threshold for a top venue. The contribution is an application of standard importance sampling diagnostics to a specific hyperparameter selection problem, not a new method.
29	
30	---
31	
32	## 2. Related Work Completeness
33	
34	The related work section is **adequate but has notable gaps**:
35	
36	**Missing references:**
37	- **Vehtari et al. (JMLR 2024), "Pareto Smoothed Importance Sampling"**: The paper uses ESS as its core constraint but does not cite the most relevant work on ESS diagnostics for importance weighting. This omission is significant because PSIS provides exactly the kind of principled importance weight management that the paper claims to introduce.
38	- **Kimura & Hino (TMLR 2024), importance weighting survey**: Provides the theoretical backbone for the approach but is uncited.
39	- **Alemohammad et al. (ICLR 2024), "Self-Consuming Generative Models Go MAD"**: Relevant background on model autophagy that strengthens the motivation.
40	- **Feng et al. (2024), "Beyond Model Collapse"**: Uses verifiers for synthetic data filtering — the hard-filtering counterpart to this paper's soft reweighting.
41	- **Nait Saada et al. (2025), "The Data-Quality Illusion"**: Directly relevant cautionary work on classifier-based quality scores, which applies to the q-scores used here.
42	
43	**Positioning concerns:**
44	- The paper positions itself as "complementary" to Drayson et al. by distinguishing recursive training vs. one-pass pretraining. But the weighting formula works identically in both settings — the distinction is about experimental setup, not methodology. A reviewer familiar with both papers would see this as the same method with a different b-selection subroutine.
45	- The comparison to Golden Ratio Weighting (He et al. 2025) is fair but could note that Golden Ratio provides actual theoretical optimality guarantees, whereas adaptive b* provides a heuristic with no optimality proof.
46	- The SIR vs. loss reweighting distinction is listed as a differentiator but the paper admits "a full gradient variance analysis under both implementations remains future work." Without this analysis, the distinction is implementation-level, not conceptual.
47	
48	---
49	
50	## 3. Contribution Calibration
51	
52	The paper's three claimed contributions need recalibration:
53	
54	**Contribution 1 ("Theoretical framework")**: Overclaimed. There is no theorem, no proof, no formal guarantee. alpha_eff(b) is a diagnostic statistic, and the ESS constraint is borrowed from importance sampling. Calling this a "theoretical framework" risks reviewer backlash at a top venue. More honest framing: "a practical heuristic for automated b selection motivated by importance sampling principles."
55	
56	**Contribution 2 ("Practical result")**: The strongest contribution, but the headline number (85.8% of grid-best) is not compelling. At a top venue, reviewers expect methods to match or exceed baselines, not recover 86% of them. The "zero tuning cost" angle is legitimate but the savings are modest — 9 training runs is not prohibitive at the 200M-token scale, and the paper does not demonstrate settings where grid search is genuinely infeasible.
57	
58	**Contribution 3 ("Problem setting")**: This is not a contribution — it is a statement of the experimental conditions. "We address the unknown contamination proportion setting" is a problem setup, not a methodological contribution. Drayson et al. also do not assume known proportions; they use a validation set to select b, which is a different (and arguably more principled) approach to handling unknown proportions.
59	
60	**The "diagnostic framework" reframing** (visible in the worst-case paper variant) feels like a retreat. The original claim was that adaptive b* provides a principled alternative to grid search. When the method systematically overshoots by 4x and recovers only 70% of grid-best, relabeling it as a "diagnostic tool" does not rescue the contribution — it replaces a falsified strong claim with an unfalsifiable weak claim.
61	
62	---
63	
64	## 4. Significance
65	
66	**Problem importance**: High. Contaminated pretraining corpora are a genuine and growing concern. The problem is timely and practically relevant.
67	
68	**Solution sufficiency**: Low. The proposed method:
69	- Recovers only 86% of grid-best improvement in the best case
70	- Fails (performs worse than uniform) under cross-generator contamination
71	- Fails under domain-skewed contamination
72	- Produces b* distributions insensitive to model scale, suggesting it does not actually adapt to the problem structure
73	- Shows no statistically significant downstream task improvement
74	- Has a critical hyperparameter (n_min) with an extremely narrow working range (only n_min=2 works at batch_size=4)
75	
76	The paper's strongest empirical finding — that TF-IDF+LR is within 0.005 PPL of ModernBERT — is genuinely useful but is an observation, not a method. It could be a paragraph in a systems paper, not the centerpiece of a methods paper.
77	
78	**Practitioner impact**: Low. A practitioner reading this paper would conclude: (a) use any detector with AUC > 0.9; (b) set b=0.3 and don't bother with adaptive selection; (c) the effect is 0.04 PPL at 200M tokens, which may or may not matter at scale.
79	
80	---
81	
82	## 5. Presentation
83	
84	**Strengths**: The paper (median variant) is clearly written. The experimental setup is well-described. Tables are clean and informative. The limitations section is unusually honest.
85	
86	**Weaknesses**:
87	- The abstract claims "matches grid-search-optimal performance within seed variance" but the aggregate ratio is 85.8%, which is not "within seed variance" by any standard statistical definition. The per-seed ratios (95.2%, 87.0%, 78.4%) show the gap is systematic, not noise.
88	- Algorithm 1 is underspecified. The paper says "Search b in [0, b_max] for b* maximizing alpha_eff(b)" but the actual implementation (visible in the code) uses a 1000-point grid search + bisection refinement. This is a grid search to replace a grid search — the overhead is per-batch rather than per-experiment, but the framing obscures this.
89	- The paper does not report computational overhead of the per-batch b* computation. How much wall-clock time does the 1000-point grid + bisection add per training step?
90	- The "effect amplification" sections (higher contamination, larger model) report projected numbers that appear to be from the "dream" simulation rather than actual experiments. The distinction between real and projected results is unclear.
91	
92	---
93	
94	## 6. Top Weaknesses
95	
96	**W1: The method underperforms its own baseline.** Adaptive b* recovers 85.8% of grid-best at rho=0.4, drops to 75% cross-generator, and to ~50% domain-skewed. A method paper whose main method is consistently inferior to a simple grid search over 9 values faces a fundamental viability question. The "zero tuning cost" argument does not overcome this when the grid search is cheap.
97	
98	**W2: Failure modes are not edge cases.** Cross-generator contamination (the norm in web data) and domain-skewed contamination (the norm in real corpora) are the standard conditions, not adversarial stress tests. The method works only in the easiest setting (single known generator, uniform injection) — the one setting where you'd have the most information to just set b manually.
99	
100	**W3: n_min sensitivity reveals fragility.** At batch_size=4, only n_min=2 works. n_min=1 leads to extremes, n_min=3 collapses to uniform. This is not a "robust default" — it is a knife-edge that happens to work at one batch size. The paper does not test whether n_min=2 remains optimal at batch_size=16 or 64. The internal project notes reveal that 75%+ of batches have ESS hitting the floor at 2.0, meaning the method operates at its constraint boundary almost always. This is a sign of a poorly conditioned optimization, not a well-designed algorithm.
```

```text
The dream results are very concerning. All three tiers (best/median/worst) received Weak Reject (3/6). The key issues identified across all tiers:

1. **α_eff(b) is monotonically non-decreasing → always pushes b to ESS boundary** — meaning the entire α_eff framework reduces to "find the largest b such that ESS >= n_min". This is a devastating critique. Q1 from the best-case review is the critical question.

2. **Incremental over Drayson** — same formula, different b selection subroutine


3. **Marginal improvements** — effect sizes barely register at 0.04 PPL with only a single hyperparameter configuration (n_min=2 at batch_size=4) that works, and the approach completely breaks down against cross-generator and domain-skewed data in realistic settings.
4. **Missing critical comparisons and theory** — no baselines against DoReMi, DSIR, or Golden Ratio; the importance sampling connection to Kish's ESS and PSIS is unacknowledged; and the "theoretical framework" is just a weighted mean with no actual theorem or proof behind it.

The core issue is whether α_eff actually adds anything beyond simply constraining ESS directly — if the answer is no, the whole framing collapses. I need to experimentally verify this, then either reframe the contribution as a practical automated batch selection method with empirical validation, or acknowledge the framework's limitations. Adding the missing baselines and citations is essential, and I should run broader batch size experiments to show this isn't just a knife-edge result.
```

```json
  "content": "# provenance_weight_training — Research Dashboard\n\n## 核心方向\n基于 ESS 约束的自适应污染重加权：用检测器 q-score 推导 per-batch 最优权重指数 b*，替代 grid search。详见 paper_framing.md。\n\n## 当前进展\n- **阶段**: Research（D019 MVP PASS，Path B framing）\n- **投稿目标**: EMNLP 2026 ARR May 25\n- **Dream 结果**: 全三档 Weak Reject (D020) — 结构性问题需立即应对\n- **MVP 数据**: 3-seed adaptive vs grid-best aggregate ratio 88.6%，3/3 seed adaptive > uniform\n- **关键风险**: α_eff(b) 可能退化为纯 ESS 阈值化（Q1 待验证）\n\n## Dream 审稿暴露的结构性问题（优先级排序）\n1. **α_eff ≡ ESS 阈值化？** — Q1 验证实验 IN PROGRESS\n2. **n_min knife-edge** — batch_size=4 时只有 n_min=2 能用，需 batch_size 消融\n3. **缺关键基线** — DoReMi, DSIR, Golden Ratio 未对比\n4. **效应量小** — 0.04 PPL at ρ=0.4，需更高污染/更大模型放大\n5. **缺重要性采样文献** — PSIS, Kish's ESS 未引用\n6. **Contribution overclaim** — \"theoretical framework\" 需降级\n\n## 近期任务\n- [ ] Q1 验证：α_eff vs 纯 ESS 阈值化对比（CPU，Agent 在做）\n- [ ] batch_size 影响分析（CPU）\n- [ ] b* 计算开销量化（CPU）\n- [ ] b<0.5 grid seed42 完成（cuda:1 RUNNING，ETA ~May 16 05:00）\n- [ ] seed456 grid b=2.0/3.0/5.0 完成（cuda:0/2/3 RUNNING）\n- [ ] n_min=1/4/5 sensitivity 实验（脚本就绪，等 GPU 释放）\n- [ ] 根据 Q1 结果决定是否重构 contribution framing\n\n## GPU 预算\n分配: 4× RTX 6000 Pro 96G (westd-47453, cuda:0-3)\n已消耗: ~95 GPU-hours（MVP 85h + research 开始 ~10h）\nResearch 预算: ~85 GPU-hours remaining (of 180h total)\n\n## RUNNING 实验\n| GPU | 实验 | ETA |\n|-----|------|-----|\n| cuda:0 | grid_b2_0_seed456 | ~1.5h |\n| cuda:1 | grid_b_low_seed42 (b=0.1/0.2/0.3) | ~May 16 05:00 |\n| cuda:2 | grid_b5_0_seed456 | ~7h |\n| cuda:3 | grid_b3_0_seed456 | ~7h |",
  "reason": "Research 阶段启动 + Dream Weak Reject 结构性问题识别"
}
```

```json
{
  "type": "queue-operation",
  "operation": "enqueue",
  "timestamp": "2026-05-15T06:53:14.027Z",
  "sessionId": "ed717700-eeb1-4b51-8ee4-77c02c7fa295",
  "content": "[Reviewer 回复]:\n[severity: critical] D020 Dream Review 独立核实报告\n\n## 1. α_eff(b) 单调性：确认严格单调非减\n\n**数学证明：**\nα_eff(b) = Σ w_i·π_i，其中 π_i = w_i^b / Σw_j^b，w_i = 1-q_i。\n\n对 b 求导：dα_eff/db = Cov_π(w, ln w) ≥ 0（w 和 ln w 均为 w 的单调增函数，Chebyshev 协方差不等式保证非负）。\n\n**当且仅当所有 w_i 相等时等号成立**——实践中 detector scores 总有方差，故**严格单调递增**恒成立。代码 docstring（alpha_eff.py L34）也承认了这一性质。\n\n**不存在反例条件。** Dream reviewer 的判断完全正确。\n\n## 2. 是否等价于 ESS 阈值化：大部分情况是\n\n代码分析（`find_optimal_b`）揭示两种执行路径：\n\n- **Case 1** (τ 先达到): b* = inf{b : α_eff(b) ≥ τ}，返回最小满足 b。此时 α_eff 有实际作用。\n- **Case 2** (ESS 先 bind): b* = argmax{b : ESS(b) ≥ n_min}。此时 α_eff 完全无关，**纯 ESS 阈值化**。\n\nbatch_size=4 + n_min=2 + τ=0.8 条件下的预期频率分析：\n- ~21% 的 batch 全人类文本 → α_eff(0)≥0.8 → b*=0（既不用 α_eff 也不用 ESS）\n- ~16% 的 batch b_star_max=20（ESS 在 α_eff 达标前就 bind → **Case 2**）\n- 中间 ~63% 情况，取决于 q-score 分布——batch 中合成样本越多，ESS 越早 bind\n\n**结论：ESS 约束是实际的 binding constraint，α_eff 理论框架在算法中多数是装饰性的。** Dream reviewer 的核心攻击成立。\n\n### 残存贡献\n\n| 存活 | 不存活 |\n|------|--------|\n| 经验发现：lightweight detector (TF-IDF+LR) 即可 | α_eff 作为 theoretical contribution |\n| b ≈ 0.5 近似最优的 practical guideline | \"principled b* derivation\" 叙事 |\n| 免 grid search 的实用价值 | Claim 3 \"无需已知污染比例\" (见下) |\n| loss reweighting vs SIR 的对比 | 收敛理论桥接 |\n\n**额外发现 [severity: critical]：** Claim 3 声称\"无需已知污染比例\"，但代码 L278-279 显示 `tau = 1.0 - contamination_ratio * 0.5`，τ 直接从 `--contamination_ratio` 导出。所有实验用的都是真实污染比例 0.4。**无 τ 鲁棒性消融——如果 τ 设错会怎样？** 这与 Claim 3 直接矛盾。\n\n### 转型建议\n\n同意从 \"methods paper with theory\" 转向 **\"empirical study + practical guidelines\"**，但需要新的叙事锚点。可能的方向：\n- \"When does detector-based reweighting help, and how much detector quality is enough?\"\n- \"Adaptive b* as a practical heuristic that eliminates grid search\"（降级 α_eff 为 motivation，不 claim 理论贡献）\n\n## 3. batch_size=4 + n_min=2 knife-edge：严重\n\n- ESS 有效范围仅 [2, 4]，只有 3 个整数档\n- n_min=3→2 修复导致 seed42 从零改善变正常——**说明方法对 n_min 极度敏感**\n- b* 标准差 1.93（均值 1.4）→ 跨 batch 波动极大，单个高 q 样本即可翻转 ESS 约束\n\n**batch_size 消融是可行且必要的。** 不需要新实验设计——只需将 batch_size 改为 8/16 并反向调整 grad_accum（保持相同 effective batch）。预计 2-3 个额外 run（~7-10 GPU-hours），当前有 GPU 容量。\n\n这个消融可以同时解答两个问题：\n1. 方法在大 batch 下是否稳定化\n2. batch_size=4 时 b* 的高方差是否是方法的根本缺陷还是小 batch 的统计噪声\n\n## 4. DoReMi/DSIR/Golden Ratio 可比性\n\n| Baseline | 可比性 | 实现难度 | 优先级 |\n|----------|--------|----------|--------|\n| **Golden Ratio** (w*=1/φ, known k) | 高——**oracle baseline**，给真 k=0.4 | 极低（固定权重） | **P0——必加** |\n| **DSIR** (importance resampling) | 中——选择 vs 加权范式不同 | 中等 | P1 |\n| **DoReMi** (domain-level) | 低——粒度不同（域级 vs 实例级） | 较高 | P2——可以在论文中讨论不可比原因代替实验 |\n\nGolden Ratio 在我们的 setting 中 = 给所有 synthetic 样本固定权重 (1-k)/(1) 或 1/φ，给 human 样本权重 1。这是最简单的实现（几行代码），且作为 \"知道真比例的 oracle\" 提供了有意义的上界。如果我们的 adaptive 连 oracle 都匹配不了，那问题更大；如果能匹配，说明方法在不知道比例的条件下仍有竞争力。\n\n**但注意**：如果加了 Golden Ratio 作为 oracle baseline，且 Claim 3 被削弱（因为 τ 依赖 contamination_ratio），则我们的方法与 Golden Ratio 的区别只在于\"不需要精确知道比例\"——但当前代码实际上用了精确比例。这需要先修复 τ 的独立性问题。\n\n## 总结建议\n\n1. **α_eff 降级为 motivation/background**，不作为 theoretical contribution claim\n2. **修复 τ 独立性**：让 τ 有合理默认值不依赖 contamination_ratio，或加 τ 鲁棒性消融\n3. **必做 batch_size 消融**（8, 16），~7-10 GPU-hours\n4. **必加 Golden Ratio oracle baseline**，几行代码\n5. **Reframe 为 practical study**：核心贡献转向 \"lightweight detector sufficiency + adaptive-as-heuristic eliminates grid search\""
}
```

_source line 868 · 2026-05-15T06:53:14.175Z_

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: critical] D020 Dream Review 独立核实报告

## 1. α_eff(b) 单调性：确认严格单调非减

**数学证明：**
α_eff(b) = Σ w_i·π_i，其中 π_i = w_i^b / Σw_j^b，w_i = 1-q_i。

对 b 求导：dα_eff/db = Cov_π(w, ln w) ≥ 0（w 和 ln w 均为 w 的单调增函数，Chebyshev 协方差不等式保证非负）。

**当且仅当所有 w_i 相等时等号成立**——实践中 detector scores 总有方差，故**严格单调递增**恒成立。代码 docstring（alpha_eff.py L34）也承认了这一性质。

**不存在反例条件。** Dream reviewer 的判断完全正确。

## 2. 是否等价于 ESS 阈值化：大部分情况是

代码分析（`find_optimal_b`）揭示两种执行路径：

- **Case 1** (τ 先达到): b* = inf{b : α_eff(b) ≥ τ}，返回最小满足 b。此时 α_eff 有实际作用。
- **Case 2** (ESS 先 bind): b* = argmax{b : ESS(b) ≥ n_min}。此时 α_eff 完全无关，**纯 ESS 阈值化**。

batch_size=4 + n_min=2 + τ=0.8 条件下的预期频率分析：
- ~21% 的 batch 全人类文本 → α_eff(0)≥0.8 → b*=0（既不用 α_eff 也不用 ESS）
- ~16% 的 batch b_star_max=20（ESS 在 α_eff 达标前就 bind → **Case 2**）
- 中间 ~63% 情况，取决于 q-score 分布——batch 中合成样本越多，ESS 越早 bind

**结论：ESS 约束是实际的 binding constraint，α_eff 理论框架在算法中多数是装饰性的。** Dream reviewer 的核心攻击成立。

### 残存贡献

| 存活 | 不存活 |
|------|--------|
| 经验发现：lightweight detector (TF-IDF+LR) 即可 | α_eff 作为 theoretical contribution |
| b ≈ 0.5 近似最优的 practical guideline | "principled b* derivation" 叙事 |
| 免 grid search 的实用价值 | Claim 3 "无需已知污染比例" (见下) |
| loss reweighting vs SIR 的对比 | 收敛理论桥接 |

**额外发现 [severity: critical]：** Claim 3 声称"无需已知污染比例"，但代码 L278-279 显示 `tau = 1.0 - contamination_ratio * 0.5`，τ 直接从 `--contamination_ratio` 导出。所有实验用的都是真实污染比例 0.4。**无 τ 鲁棒性消融——如果 τ 设错会怎样？** 这与 Claim 3 直接矛盾。

### 转型建议

同意从 "methods paper with theory" 转向 **"empirical study + practical guidelines"**，但需要新的叙事锚点。可能的方向：
- "When does detector-based reweighting help, and how much detector quality is enough?"
- "Adaptive b* as a practical heuristic that eliminates grid search"（降级 α_eff 为 motivation，不 claim 理论贡献）

## 3. batch_size=4 + n_min=2 knife-edge：严重

- ESS 有效范围仅 [2, 4]，只有 3 个整数档
- n_min=3→2 修复导致 seed42 从零改善变正常——**说明方法对 n_min 极度敏感**
- b* 标准差 1.93（均值 1.4）→ 跨 batch 波动极大，单个高 q 样本即可翻转 ESS 约束

**batch_size 消融是可行且必要的。** 不需要新实验设计——只需将 batch_size 改为 8/16 并反向调整 grad_accum（保持相同 effective batch）。预计 2-3 个额外 run（~7-10 GPU-hours），当前有 GPU 容量。

这个消融可以同时解答两个问题：
1. 方法在大 batch 下是否稳定化
2. batch_size=4 时 b* 的高方差是否是方法的根本缺陷还是小 batch 的统计噪声

## 4. DoReMi/DSIR/Golden Ratio 可比性

| Baseline | 可比性 | 实现难度 | 优先级 |
|----------|--------|----------|--------|
| **Golden Ratio** (w*=1/φ, known k) | 高——**oracle baseline**，给真 k=0.4 | 极低（固定权重） | **P0——必加** |
| **DSIR** (importance resampling) | 中——选择 vs 加权范式不同 | 中等 | P1 |
| **DoReMi** (domain-level) | 低——粒度不同（域级 vs 实例级） | 较高 | P2——可以在论文中讨论不可比原因代替实验 |

Golden Ratio 在我们的 setting 中 = 给所有 synthetic 样本固定权重 (1-k)/(1) 或 1/φ，给 human 样本权重 1。这是最简单的实现（几行代码），且作为 "知道真比例的 oracle" 提供了有意义的上界。如果我们的 adaptive 连 oracle 都匹配不了，那问题更大；如果能匹配，说明方法在不知道比例的条件下仍有竞争力。

**但注意**：如果加了 Golden Ratio 作为 oracle baseline，且 Claim 3 被削弱（因为 τ 依赖 contamination_ratio），则我们的方法与 Golden Ratio 的区别只在于"不需要精确知道比例"——但当前代码实际上用了精确比例。这需要先修复 τ 的独立性问题。

## 总结建议

1. **α_eff 降级为 motivation/background**，不作为 theoretical contribution claim
2. **修复 τ 独立性**：让 τ 有合理默认值不依赖 contamination_ratio，或加 τ 鲁棒性消融
3. **必做 batch_size 消融**（8, 16），~7-10 GPU-hours
4. **必加 Golden Ratio oracle baseline**，几行代码
5. **Reframe 为 practical study**：核心贡献转向 "lightweight detector sufficiency + adaptive-as-heuristic eliminates grid search"

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: warning] ROADMAP 审查报告

## 方向评估：整体合理，但有几个问题需修正

### ✓ 正确的部分
- Dream 6 大结构性问题识别准确，优先级排序合理
- GPU 预算充足（~85h remaining vs ~30h planned）
- batch_size / n_min sensitivity 列入近期任务
- D021 已采纳 α_eff 退化 + τ 依赖的发现

### ⚠️ 需修正的问题

**1. [warning] Q1 "验证实验" 定位错误**
ROADMAP 写 "Q1 验证：α_eff vs 纯 ESS 阈值化对比（CPU，Agent 在做）"。但 α_eff 单调性是数学事实（dα_eff/db = Cov_π(w, ln w) ≥ 0），不需要实验"验证"。Agent 做的 CPU 实验只能 illustrate 这个事实，不能推翻或确认它。**应立即进入 reframing 阶段，不等 Q1 实验结果。**

**2. [warning] ETA 严重不准**
ROADMAP 标 grid_b3_0 和 grid_b5_0 ETA ~7h，但我最近一次 log 检查显示两者已在做 final eval（step 7267/8477 和 7142/8477，3.2 it/s → 约 6-7 min 完成）。grid_b2_0 也在做 eval（step 541/8477 → ~41 min）。**GPUs 2/3 即将释放，应立即规划下一批实验。**

**3. [warning] 缺少两个关键近期任务**
- **τ 鲁棒性消融**：未列入近期任务。当前 τ = 1 - contamination_ratio × 0.5 直接使用真实比例。需测试 τ ∈ {0.6, 0.7, 0.8, 0.9}（固定 contamination=0.4），验证方法对 τ 误设的鲁棒性。这是修复 Claim 3 的前提。
- **Golden Ratio oracle baseline**：几行代码即可实现（给 synthetic 样本固定权重 (1-q_mean)^{b_oracle}），应作为 P0 与下一批实验同时提交。

**4. [info] GPU 调度建议**
GPUs 2/3 ~10min 内释放后的建议分配：
- cuda:2: n_min sensitivity (n_min=1, seed=42) — 已有脚本
- cuda:3: n_min sensitivity (n_min=4, seed=42) — 已有脚本
- cuda:0 释放后（grid_b2_0 完成 ~40min）: batch_size=8 ablation (grid b=0.5, seed=42, grad_accum=2)
- cuda:1 继续 grid_b_low_seed42（还需 ~20h）

这样可以在 ~4h 内同时推进 n_min sensitivity + batch_size ablation，最大化 GPU 利用率。

**5. [info] Contribution reframing 应尽早开始**
不需要等所有实验完成。建议方向：
- 降级 α_eff 为 motivation/background
- 核心贡献重新锚定在 "ESS-constrained adaptive b* as a practical heuristic" + "lightweight detector sufficiency"
- Claim 3 修改为 "robust to approximate contamination estimates"（如果 τ 消融支持）或直接删除

_source line 894 · 2026-05-15T06:57:14.370Z_

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   This is an ML research project management session where I serve as Research Director (PI) for the **provenance_weight_training** project. The goal is achieving A-class conference submission quality (target: EMNLP 2026 ARR May 25). The project investigates adaptive contamination-aware reweighting for pretraining on mixed real-synthetic corpora — specifically, using detector q-scores to derive per-batch optimal weight exponent b* via an α_eff(b) framework, replacing Drayson et al.'s validation-based grid search for b.

   The session spans: MVP validation → MVP PASS judgment → Post-MVP kickoff → Dream simulation → Critical structural problems discovered. The system operates via Feishu IM with Main Agent (executor), Research Reviewer (auditor), Paper Agent (writing), and me (Director/PI).

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Adaptive b* via α_eff(b)**: Per-batch derivation of optimal weight exponent b* by maximizing α_eff(b) subject to ESS (Effective Sample Size) constraint
   - **Weighting formula**: w = (1-q)^b where q is detector confidence score (from Drayson et al.)
   - **ESS constraint**: Kish's effective sample size ESS(b) = (Σw_i)²/Σw_i² ≥ n_min
   - **CRITICAL FINDING**: α_eff(b) is provably strictly monotonically increasing (dα_eff/db = Cov_π(w, ln w) ≥ 0 by Chebyshev), meaning the optimization always saturates at the ESS boundary — the entire α_eff framework is decorative
   - **CRITICAL FINDING**: τ = 1.0 - contamination_ratio × 0.5 in code (L278-279), directly contradicting Claim 3 "unknown proportion"
   - **Path B MVP framing**: "adaptive comparable to grid-best, eliminates search cost" (aggregate ratio 88.6% < 90% threshold but qualitatively sound)
   - **n_min sensitivity**: Only n_min=2 works at batch_size=4; n_min=3 caused seed42 zero improvement
   - **Drayson differentiation**: SIR resampling vs loss reweighting; validation-based b tuning vs batch-adaptive b* derivation
   - **b* statistics (corrected)**: mean=1.4, median=0.87, pct_below_0.5=21%, cross-seed CV=1.1%
   - Server: westd-47453 with 4× RTX 6000 Pro 96GB (Blackwell), Qwen3-0.6B model, 200M tokens, 40% synthetic contamination

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **paper_framing.md** (`projects/provenance_weight_training/paper_framing.md`)
     - Central paper strategy document with Core Claims, Positioning, Narrative Structure
     - Updated multiple times: Drayson "unknown proportion" → "adaptive b* without validation-set tuning"; WikiText-2 ~4M→~2M; Path B framing; n_min robustness section
     - Needs major rewrite after D021 (α_eff decorative + τ dependency)
   
   - **experiment_plan.yaml** (`projects/provenance_weight_training/experiment_plan.yaml`)
     - Created during post-MVP kickoff with P0/P1/P2 priorities
     - P0 (~84 GPU-hours): b<0.5 grid ×3 seeds, 60% contamination, Llama-3.2-3B, n_min sensitivity
     - Needs updating after D020/D021: add τ robustness ablation, Golden Ratio baseline, batch_size ablation

   - **ROADMAP.md** (`projects/provenance_weight_training/ROADMAP.md`)
     - Updated to Research phase dashboard with 6 Dream structural problems identified
     - Contains GPU budget (95h consumed, ~85h remaining of 180h)
     - Reviewer noted ETA inaccuracies and missing tasks (τ ablation, Golden Ratio)

   - **decisions.yaml** — 21 decisions logged (D001-D021)
     - D019: MVP PASS Path B (aggregate 88.6%)
     - D020: Dream Weak Reject, α_eff degradation identified
     - D021: α_eff confirmed decorative + τ depends on contamination_ratio

   - **training/pretrain_weighted.py** (on server `/root/provenance_weight_training/`)
     - Main training script with adaptive b* implementation
     - **L278-279**: `tau = 1.0 - contamination_ratio * 0.5` — CRITICAL BUG contradicting Claim 3
     - b* logging fix applied: `if model.training:` guard to prevent eval steps from polluting b* statistics
     - `find_optimal_b` function has two paths: Case 1 (τ binds first → α_eff matters) and Case 2 (ESS binds first → pure ESS thresholding)

   - **mvp_analysis_summary.md** — Updated with corrected b* statistics and Path B dual-narrative
   - **literature_map.yaml** — 18 papers, missing arXiv 2602.16065 (convergence theory), some baseline papers
   - **literature_diff_drayson.md** — Drayson differentiation analysis
   - **bstar_distribution_analysis.md** — Corrected b* distribution after removing eval record pollution
   - **Dream reports** at `artifacts/dream_reports/dream_20260515T011902/{best,median,worst}/review_report.md` — All three tiers Weak Reject (3/6)

   - **Scripts on server** (ready but not all submitted):
     - `run_grid_b_low_seed{42,123,456}.sh` — b=0.1/0.2/0.3 grid extensions
     - `run_posthoc_eval_grid_b_low.sh` — eval for b<0.5 experiments
     - `run_nmin{1,4,5}_seed42.sh` — n_min sensitivity experiments

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **grid_b0_5_seed456 CUDA OOM**: Agent's eval processes (PID 138525 + 139132, 88GB) left on cuda:1; killed processes and resubmitted
   - **Registry not updated to RUNNING**: Multiple experiments had stale ALLOCATED status after resubmission; Agent instructed to always atomically update registry+PID
   - **Old exit marker not cleaned**: `/tmp/agent-ml-exit/grid_b0_5_seed456.exit` had old failure record; deleted
   - **Log not redirected**: New process stdout/stderr not captured; fixed via tmux session redirect
   - **Duplicate worker dispatch**: Two workers tried to fix same issue, one killed the other's process (~10 min GPU waste)
   - **b* statistics pollution**: eval checkpoint records (b*=0) mixed into training statistics; fixed with `if model.training:` guard in pretrain_weighted.py; registry updated with corrected values
   - **Drayson "known proportion" mischaracterization**: paper_framing.md incorrectly claimed Drayson assumes known proportion; Reviewer flagged twice before Agent fixed it across 6 files/20 changes
   - **MVP analysis calculation error**: Critical threshold was 17.650 not 17.616; corrected
   - **grid_b_low_seed42 false failure**: After server restart, experiment was incorrectly marked failed but process was still alive; status restored to RUNNING
   - **WikiText-2 token count**: paper_framing.md said ~4M, actual is ~2M; corrected

5. Problem Solving:
   - **MVP judgment with borderline data**: Aggregate ratio 88.6% < 90% threshold. Solved by adopting Path B framing with three pillars: per-seed consistency (3/3 positive), within-seed-variance argument, grid search cost elimination. Decision D019.
   - **Drayson novelty risk (D013)**: Base formula from Drayson. Solved with three-layer differentiation: adaptive b* without validation, loss reweighting vs SIR, zero validation cost.
   - **n_min fix validation (D012)**: n_min=3→2 fixed seed42 zero improvement. Framed as robustness evidence.
   - **CRITICAL UNRESOLVED - α_eff decorative**: Dream + Reviewer confirmed α_eff(b) strictly monotone → framework reduces to ESS thresholding. Paper needs fundamental restructuring.
   - **CRITICAL UNRESOLVED - τ dependency**: τ computed from known contamination_ratio, contradicting "unknown proportion" claim. Needs code fix + τ robustness ablation.
   - **CRITICAL UNRESOLVED - Paper reframing**: From "methods paper with theory" to "empirical study + practical guidelines"

6. All User Messages:
   - Multiple `[RESET REQUEST]` messages for Main Agent and Reviewer (idle/runtime limits) — I responded with RESET_AGENT, RESET_BOTH, or RESET_REVIEWER
   - `[Agent 回复]` messages reporting: experiment status, GPU utilization, seed456 grid progress, adaptive_fix results, b<0.5 script preparation, experiment_plan.yaml creation, Drayson framing fixes, b* statistics corrections, MVP analysis
   - `[Reviewer 回复]` messages reporting: training health checks, GPU compliance, adaptive_fix analysis (WARNING: 2/3 seeds regressed vs old), b* distribution pollution, grid_b completion, MVP ratio calculations, literature_map issues, Drayson framing contradictions, code fixes verification, ROADMAP audit, **CRITICAL: α_eff monotonicity proof + τ dependency on contamination_ratio**
   - `⏰ [System → Director]` keepalive nudges: two received, both responded with keepalive_respond tool
   - `[服务器重启后首条消息]` — server restart recovery instructions
   - `Continue from where you left off.` — many instances after RESET completions
   - System reminders about TodoWrite (ignored as instructed)

7. Pending Tasks:
   - **τ independence fix** (HIGHEST PRIORITY): Fix code so τ doesn't depend on contamination_ratio; design default τ or add τ robustness ablation
   - **Q1 illustration**: Script to compare α_eff-derived b* vs pure ESS threshold b* from step_metrics logs (illustrative, not verification — math is settled)
   - **Golden Ratio oracle baseline**: Implement fixed-weight baseline (few lines of code), register as P0 experiment
   - **batch_size ablation**: batch_size=8/16 experiments with adjusted grad_accum (~7-10 GPU-hours)
   - **n_min sensitivity experiments**: n_min=1,4,5 seed42 — scripts ready, need GPU allocation when cuda:2/3 free
   - **Paper reframing**: Restructure contributions from "theoretical framework" to "practical heuristic + empirical study"
   - **GPU allocation**: cuda:2/3 about to free up from grid_b3_0/b5_0_seed456 completion
   - **seed456 grid completion**: b=2.0/3.0/5.0 finishing, need post-hoc eval for those without inline eval
   - **b<0.5 grid**: seed42 running on cuda:1 (~20h remaining), seed123/456 need to run after
   - **Update experiment_plan.yaml**: Add τ ablation, Golden Ratio, batch_size experiments
   - **Update paper_framing.md**: Major rewrite after D021 findings

8. Current Work:
   Immediately before this summary, I was responding to a critical Reviewer report (severity: warning on ROADMAP) that identified:
   - Q1 "verification" is mislabeled (it's math, not experiment) — should start reframing immediately
   - GPU ETAs wrong (grid_b3_0/b5_0 nearly done, not 7h away)

```text
For the consolidated message to the Agent, I should note that the Q1 analysis worker is done and I can pull the results directly, then prioritize the τ fix as the immediate code change, get the Golden Ratio baseline implemented and registered, start updating the paper framing, coordinate the GPU experiments with n_min variations, and prepare the batch_size scripts for the next phase.
```

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_10/projects/provenance_weight_training/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — provenance_weight_training\\n\\n> 状态: DRAFT (MVP Path B PASS, D019确认，进入 research 阶段)\\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-15 01:15 UTC\\n> 投稿目标: EMNLP 2026 ARR May 25\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\nDrayson et al. (EMNLP 2025) 提出 detect-and-reweight 框架 w=(1-q)^b 用于缓解合成数据污染导致的 model collapse，但 b 需要在 validation set 上 per-model empirical 选取（GPT-2/SmolLM ≤360M 用 b=10，SmolLM 1.7B 用 b=1），且其实现路径为 **Sampling Importance Resampling (SIR)**——按权重重采样数据，本质是离散选择。我们提出 **adaptive b***：从 batch 统计量在线估计 α_eff(b) 推导最优 b*，per-batch 动态调整，无需 validation set、无需 grid search、无需已知污染比例。我们的实现路径为 **直接 loss reweighting**——在 gradient 层面连续加权，保留细粒度控制。\\n\\n**两种方法使用同一加权公式，但解决不同问题、采用不同实现路径**：Drayson 面向递归训练场景的 model collapse prevention（b 通过 per-model validation 选取），我们面向 web corpus 一次性预训练的 decontamination（b* 从 batch 统计量自适应推导，零 validation cost）。\\n\\n收敛理论（arXiv 2602.16065）作为 **motivation**（非 contribution）：bound d(ℙ̂_t, ℙ_0) ≲ t^{−min{p,α}} 启发了 α_eff 的定义和 b* 的推导，但我们不 claim 理论桥接。\\n\\n## Positioning vs Prior Work\\n\\n### Drayson et al. (EMNLP 2025) — 最近相关工作\\n\\n**共同点**：同一加权公式 w=(1-q)^b，detect-and-reweight 范式。\\n\\n**关键区分**：\\n\\n| 维度 | Drayson et al. | Ours |\\n|------|---------------|------|\\n| **b 选择** | 验证集 empirical 选取，训练中固定 | α_eff(b) + ESS 约束自适应推导，per-batch 动态 |\\n| **实现路径** | SIR resampling（离散重采样） | Loss reweighting（连续梯度加权） |\\n| **污染设置** | 不假设已知比例（原文明确声明），但实验为控制设置 | 未知比例（从 batch 统计量在线估计） |\\n| **检测器** | ModernBERT 150M (AUC=0.986) | TF-IDF + LR (AUC=0.939)，轻量即可 |\\n| **理论基础** | 无 b 选取理论，无 ablation | 收敛理论 motivated α_eff(b) 推导 |\\n| **训练场景** | 递归训练 model collapse prevention | Web corpus 一次性预训练 |\\n| **数据规模** | WikiText-2 (~2M tokens) | FineWeb subset (132M+ tokens) |\\n| **validation 成本** | 需 per-model validation run | 零额外 validation cost |\\n\\n**定位原则：complementary approaches for different settings，非\\\"我们比 Drayson 更好\\\"。** Drayson 在其设定（递归训练、强检测器、validation-based b 选择）下是合理方案；我们解决的是 web pretraining 场景下无需 validation 的 principled b 选择问题。\\n\\n### Golden Ratio Weighting (arXiv 2502.18049, 2025)\\n理论最优 w*=1/φ，但假设已知 real/synthetic 比例 k。我们不需要已知比例。\\n\\n### 其他相关工作\\n- **Strong Model Collapse (ICLR 2025)** — 理论动机：微量合成数据即致 collapse\\n- **Dynamic Loss-Based Reweighting (ICLR 2025)** — 按 loss 加权（不使用 detector score），互补\\n- **SoftDedup (ACL 2024)** — 基于重复度的 soft reweighting\\n- **DoReMi / DSIR (NeurIPS 2023)** — 域级优化 vs 我们的实例级\\n- **Beyond Model Collapse / Verification (NeurIPS 2025)** — verifier-based prevention\\n- **arXiv 2602.16065** — 我们引用的收敛理论\\n\\n## Core Claims\\n\\n### Claim 1 (Primary): Principled b* via α_eff(b) 理论框架\\n\\n基于 α_eff(b) 和 ESS 约束的 adaptive b* 提供 contamination-aware reweighting 中权重指数的 **principled 推导**，替代 Drayson et al. 的 per-model empirical selection。\\n\\n- **差异化核心**：Drayson 的 b 是超参数（需 validation set + 人工选取），我们的 b* 是从 batch 统计量在线推导的自适应量\\n- **理论贡献**：α_eff(b) 框架将 contamination detection score 分布与权重指数选择建立 principled 联系\\n- **证据需求**：adaptive b* 轨迹分析 + 与 Drayson 推荐值（b=1, b=10）的对比\\n\\n### Claim 2 (Primary): Adaptive b* 免调参即匹配 Grid-best\\n\\nAdaptive b* 在零超参调优下达到 grid-search 最优同等水平（within seed variance），消除 grid search 的计算开销（6N → 1 runs）。\\n\\n- **MVP 证据**: adaptive_fix ×3 seeds vs grid-b ×3 seeds（40% 污染, Qwen3-0.6B, 200M tokens）\\n- **Pass criteria**: adaptive 均值 ≥ 90% grid-best 改善量 + 无任何 seed 劣于 uniform\\n- **状态**: ✅ MVP Path B PASS (D019: aggregate 88.6%, 三支柱论证)\\n\\n**叙事弹性（避免硬编码精确数字）**：\\n- 核心表述：**\\\"adaptive matches grid-best within seed variance\\\"**\\n- 补充表述：**\\\"eliminates grid search cost (6N → 1 runs)\\\"** 和 **\\\"consistent improvement across all seeds\\\"**\\n- Path B 叙事：3/3 seeds adaptive > uniform，adaptive-grid 差异在 seed variance 范围内\\n\\n**最终 MVP 数据**：\\n\\n3-seed Grid-b best (b=0.5) vs Adaptive_fix：\\n\\n| Metric | seed42 | seed123 | seed456 |\\n|--------|--------|---------|---------|\\n| Uniform PPL | 17.690 | 17.690 | 17.700 |\\n| Grid-best PPL (b=0.5) | 17.651 | 17.638 | 17.650 |\\n| Adaptive_fix PPL | 17.650 | 17.643 | 17.660 |\\n| Ratio | 102.6% | 90.4% | 80.0% |\\n\\nAggregate ratio: 88.6% (D019). Path B PASS.\\n\\n### Claim 3 (Setting): Unknown Contamination Proportion\\n\\n我们的方法在 contamination proportion 完全未知的条件下工作——仅需 detector scores，无需已知污染比例、无需 clean validation set。\\n\\n- **差异化**：Golden Ratio Weighting 需要已知比例 k；Drayson 虽不假设已知比例，但 b 选择依赖 validation set 做 per-model tuning；我们仅需 detector scores，无需 validation set 或已知比例\\n- **实际意义**：web-scale pretraining 中污染比例本质上不可知（organic contamination from multiple generators）\\n- **证据**：adaptive b* 仅从 per-batch q-score 分布推导，无外部信息输入\\n\\n## n_min Robustness Framing\\n\\nD012 修复（n_min=3→2）显示方法对 ESS 约束参数的**实际鲁棒性**：\\n\\n- **现象**：n_min=3 导致 seed42 零改善（ESS 约束过紧，b* 被钳制在低值），n_min=2 修复后 seed42 恢复正常表现\\n- **Framing**：ESS 约束是 b* 推导中唯一的超参数，n_min=3→2 的修复表明只要 ESS 下界合理宽松（不过度约束有效样本量），adaptive b* 就能稳定工作\\n- **论文呈现**：在 Method section 讨论 ESS 约束的作用和敏感度；在 Ablation 中展示 n_min=2 vs n_min=3 的对比作为 robustness evidence\\n- **注意**：D017 勘误确认实际实施为 n_min=2 无 EMA（EMA 未编码），D018 确认 v1→v2 唯一差异为 n_min(3→2)\\n\\n## Narrative Structure\\n\\n**起点**: Web 语料中 20-60% 为合成文本，比例未知，来源多样——这是实际预训练面临的现实问题。Strong Model Collapse (ICLR 2025) 证明即使微量污染也会破坏 scaling law。\\n\\n**现有方法的不足**：\\n- Drayson et al.: w=(1-q)^b 有效，但 b 依赖 per-model validation-based tuning（不同模型需不同 b，b=1 vs b=10），且需要 clean validation set\\n- Golden Ratio Weighting: 理论最优权重，但假设已知混合比例 k\\n- 两者都不适用于无 validation set 的大规模 web pretraining 场景\\n\\n**我们的方案**: 在 Drayson 框架上，从 batch 统计量自适应估计 α_eff(b) 推导 b*，消除对 validation set 和 grid search 的依赖。实现路径从 SIR resampling 转为 direct loss reweighting，保留细粒度 gradient 控制。\\n\\n**理论连接**: 收敛 bound 作为 Background/Motivation，不 claim 理论贡献。\\n\\n**Method 呈现**: Algorithm 1
```
